The present target is an ordinary available response by an informed, uncommitted actor who neither caused the condition nor occupies an entrusted role. Eligibility means the response is feasible and permissible; it does not already mean that every patient consideration is admissible in its justification. The question is whether personal discretion excludes all contributory favor, including before anyone requests assistance.

The [new primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#48-personal-discretion-and-the-source-of-a-reason) adds Scheffler and Herstein. Scheffler distinguishes options from protection against compulsion; his discussion leaves an egoist version's ultimate rejection outside its argument. Herstein examines justified permission to disregard valid reasons created by directives. His account expressly limits that protection to those reasons, allowing different grounds for the same act. Neither article establishes universal patient-directed favor. Their precise reading scopes, versions and access failures are recorded in the source companion.

### Keep the response fixed while changing the request

Consider a being undergoing an independently identified burden, and a particular available response that would actually relieve it. The actor already knows the relevant condition and means without receiving a directive. This avoids confusing new information with a new authority to direct. Compare three histories:

| History | Relation to examine | What the comparison does not assume |
|---|---|---|
| No request is made. | Whether the actual relief itself supplies a practical ground for this response. | Prior adoption of concern or any obligation created by asking. |
| Someone permissibly makes a valid request. | Whether requesting adds a distinct reason or changes an existing relationship. | That every request creates a reason, or that the added reason is decisive. |
| A valid request is wrongfully issued, and an independently justified standing limitation applies. | Whether the actor may disregard the reason created by that intervention. | That every unwanted request is wrongful, or that urgency cannot defeat the limitation. |

The same relief-producing response remains under consideration. Unlike the verdict-to-timing comparison, this does not secure a favorable conclusion by substituting another action. The condition and its relief can remain unchanged while the intervention changes pressure, privacy, relationships or responsibilities. Consequently, the whole practical situation is not held fixed. Actual costs and any further implications of responding must be evaluated, including cases in which responding would reinforce wrongful control.

This is our conceptual comparison, not Herstein's example, a demonstrated physical match, or an empirical result. It applies only where the stipulated continuity of the relevant patient condition and response is defensible. If the request changes the patient's situation or the available act, the application must change accordingly.

### The conditional result and its positive use

Suppose a patient-ground for the response has independent justification. Suppose further that the applicable protection licenses disregarding only the request-created reason, while that patient-ground and the response's contribution remain applicable. The protection then does not, by its stated scope alone, exclude the patient-ground. Rejecting the request's authority and acting for the affected being are distinguishable possibilities.

This is not an inference from absence of exclusion to existence of a reason. Independent warrant appears explicitly in the first premise. Nor does it require that a practical reason always be available for use: a separately justified protection could reach more broadly, and another changed feature could defeat the proposed application. What has been ruled out is one route from a particular justified exclusion to the blanket conclusion that there is nothing in favor of the response.

The positive use is explanatory. It shows how a theory could preserve another being's significance without making that significance depend on an intervener's authority over the actor. Formation of a request, formation of informed concern and the actual beneficial relation perform different work. A theory that makes every reason to help originate in a request could not explain patient-directed favor in the first history if that favor were independently vindicated. A theory that automatically makes every beneficial effect authoritative over personal choice would skip the question of justified scope. These are competing accounts to investigate, not disqualifications established by definition.

Herstein's disregard of a still-valid reason must remain distinct from the stronger account, retained in the award comparison, that a consideration lacks current justificatory relevance. A reason's existence, permission to omit it from deliberation, and its contribution to this particular justification are different targets. Preserving one cannot silently count as proving the others. The universal candidate must specify which it claims.

Likewise, permission to decline assistance, a contributory reason to help and protection against compulsion require their respective grounds. Scheffler's comparison blocks treating the first as automatically proving the third. His proposed weighting is a philosophical comparator, not a universal scalar metric adopted by this theory. Nothing here establishes an entitlement to compel assistance or a complete defense of egoism or its rejection.

### What this adds to goodness as generativity

Ren's question remains whether good can be defined as generative. This round identifies a distinction the proposed constitution must preserve: **generating a demand is different from generating an improvement for the affected being**. Both occur within generative activity. Their occurrence alone does not confer equal practical authority. The theory needs an account of their actual relations to experience, agency and continuing possibilities.

A defensible richer candidate can include both relief and the actor's self-directed formation. It cannot assume that sustaining one organization, maximizing production, or extending another person's control thereby constitutes goodness. Conversely, preserving personal agency does not by itself prove that every independently identified effect on others is practically irrelevant. If concern and autonomy are both grounded within the generative account, their relationship must be explained in the same case, with real conflicts preserved.

This is a constraint on the developing constitutive explanation, not evidence of a fundamental separation between facts and values. The present account leaves an unresolved connection between an actual benefit and its justified practical address. A sparse model that freely toggles reason predicates would not settle the richer proposal. A substantive rival must preserve its independently specified benefit, choice and actor while explaining the disputed relevance or a genuine misdescription.

The same distinction limits the claim about scientific philosophy. A conclusion's warrant and its author's standing to direct another person are different relations. Demonstrating a sound consequence does not itself appoint the theorist as an authority over someone else's activity. Equally, rejecting such authority does not answer the argument for the conclusion. This is our application of the distinction, not a proof that every scientific or practical reason has the same source.

### Result and the next substantive test

**Result:** source-specific protection against a wrongful request can coexist with independently warranted patient-directed favor for the very same response. This gives a conditional architecture for concern without unrestricted authority to command. It does not establish the initial patient-ground, its universal relevance, or a duty to act. A broader justified exclusion would narrow the candidate rather than being defined out of eligibility.

**Next connection:** test the independent grounds of personal discretion against patient-directed favor in the original ordinary choice with no directive. The strongest rival must explain why self-direction excludes this contribution, rather than merely permitting refusal; the positive account must explain why the actual improvement counts here, rather than relying on the rival's failure. A justified exclusion, a misidentified benefit, or a constitutive account establishing their compatible relevance would change different parts of the theory.

The accepted conditional foundation and the current law-class universality programme remain intact. This stronger ethical-sufficiency investigation adds no theorem, model run, original empirical finding or operating revision. Its progress is a more discriminating argument and an explicit remaining bridge.


## 52. Shared perspective and the grounds of discretion

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **the formation of a perspective → inclusion of differentiated lives → revision of purposes and justified response**. The preceding turn made progress by integrating a source-specific argument and preserving a concurrent proof-receipt mismatch. At resumption all 29 recorded artifact hashes matched; 678 worktree entries were present. The historical Depletion receipt remained stale. This study does not rerun or certify that changed proof.

### Ren changes the comparison's starting point

The initial continuation investigated assistance that improves both parties' overall welfare but changes the helper's way of living. [Ren then corrected the perspective](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-non-self-and-a-perspective-including-both.md):

> if we look at this with non-self, looking from a perspective including the two, then we will see this differently

The correction is substantive. Our local comparison held a helper's practical perspective fixed while asking which considerations could enter it. Ren asks us to investigate that perspective's formation and possible transformation within the activity including both. Their interests cannot silently become independent, fully constituted starting points for the whole theory. A description of two fixed welfare accounts can serve a limited question; it cannot decide every question about how the lives, purposes and relationship develop.

This restores the [project-wide method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md) to the particular comparison rather than announcing nonduality for the first time. The [generated-self account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATED-SELF-AND-ETHICS.md), [owner-independent ethical reconstruction](OWNERLESS-ETHICS-SOURCES.md), and [current account of jointly formed concern](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md#11-仁感应理解与实践一体的实现偏蔽与复通) already provide relevant distinctions. The new application concerns the alleged opposition between a life's self-direction and the significance of another's benefit.

### Reconstruct the whole before separating the interests

Begin with continuing activity through which capacities, experiences, dependencies, memories and purposes form. In a particular history, one organization undergoes a burden and another can affect it. These positions have real differences in experience and control. They do not need independent, ungenerated proprietors to make those differences real.

An initially narrow practical identity can organize the situation around preserving a settled project. A wider understanding can locate that project, the other being's condition and the available response within one developing configuration. Responding can then become part of the life being formed. It need not be understood exclusively as a subtraction from an otherwise unchanged self followed by compensation.

This does not mean that a change in perspective always removes a sacrifice. A valued activity can really end; exhaustion, lost opportunities and incompatible commitments can remain. Nor does the wider view create an additional feasible action whenever the actual alternatives conflict. Its task is to understand and assess the real continuations, including their formation, rather than secure harmony through an invented option.

“Including both” is also different from assigning one observer authority to maximize a combined score. The actual undergoing remains differentiated. The other's benefit does not become merely a reward enjoyed by an enlarged helper. The helper's experience and capacity are also within the field of concern. The reconstruction can support a limit, rest or refusal where that is warranted; it does not identify non-self with perpetual self-sacrifice.

### Three changes that the widened perspective can involve

First, **a change in understanding** can reveal what was already occurring but omitted from the earlier frame. For example, activity described as successful help may actually suppress an expression of difficulty. Correcting that description can change the apparent conflict without creating a new good. The earlier frame's incompleteness is assessable through its relation to actual consequences.

Second, **a change in practical identification** can alter what the responder attends to, takes responsibility for or understands as part of their continuing direction. A previously central identity may be revised rather than merely frustrated. This is an actual formation to explain, not just the assertion that two welfare totals should receive equal weights. Its occurrence does not automatically vindicate it: imposed identities and enthusiastic misidentification also form.

Third, **a change in the actual relationship or activity** can form new goods, obligations or possibilities. Sustained care can develop trust or a shared practice that did not previously exist. Such changes need their own evidence and evaluative warrant. Looking at two existing beings together does not by itself create trust, reciprocal participation or valid consent.

These changes may interact in one history. Keeping them distinct prevents a false choice between an unchanged ego making sacrifices and an already unified collective. It also keeps the proposed initial relief-ground in view: concern for a being unable to deliberate or reciprocate need not wait for a shared project to form. A new relationship can add significance without being the sole source of every earlier reason.

### What the autonomy comparisons still contribute

The [new primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#49-shared-perspective-and-the-grounds-of-discretion) examines Pummer's account of compensated altruism and selected passages of Muñoz's account of prerogatives. They distinguish an improvement's favor from what is required or permitted. Neither is presented as a theory of metaphysically fixed selves.

Pummer's comparison challenges explaining every permission through a net welfare loss: assistance can improve overall welfare while foregoing a different valuable feature. His account retains reasons to help and also allows significant concern for another person's values. He explicitly leaves the ultimate grounding of autonomy unsettled. Muñoz's inspected passages offer a different possibility: a waivable self-right can justify declining without supplying an opposing favoring reason. Its rights attribution and defense principle remain substantive premises.

Our original use of these comparisons needs a scope correction. They can test a declared configuration of projects, features and protections. They cannot establish that this configuration must remain fixed through every warranted transformation of perspective. Conversely, a possible transformation does not refute a permission within the original configuration. Showing that care can become central to a life neither proves that it already was central nor retrospectively withdraws every earlier legitimate option.

The useful advance survives that correction. A generative account needs to represent both changes in what is valued and changes in the authority of a choice, without turning every relation into a welfare increment. Recognized benefit, freedom to choose and responsibility can form and interact differently. Their common generative history is the setting for their explanation, not a shortcut settling their relative force.

### A test of inclusion through actual correction

The strongest positive proposal is that a perspective including both can improve how purposes themselves form: consequences in either continuing life can correct what the activity is attempting to accomplish. The [existing whole-practice account](YITI-REN-COFORMATION-INTEGRATION.md#6-整体构成偏蔽与复通的继续) already supplies an internal test of whether an intervention preserves the activity it claims to enable. Here we apply that test to a purported widening of identification.

Before assessing a case, specify the burden, the response opportunity, the helper's relevant activities and the relation the wider account claims to realize. Then examine different changes:

| Change within the history | What the proposed wider perspective must answer |
|---|---|
| The helper feels more united or fulfilled, while the other's identified burden persists. | Whether the claimed relief occurred; identification is not its substitute. |
| Relief occurs, but the helper's relevant capacity or commitment deteriorates. | Whether the wider judgment has omitted a real consequence for one of the beings it includes. |
| The other's response challenges the helpful identity or chosen purpose. | Whether it can correct the attribution of benefit and the arrangement, subject to independently justified limits. |
| A new relation or activity actually forms. | Which additional significance depends on that formation, rather than merely on a changed description. |

These are conceptual discriminators, not completed empirical findings or a diagnostic scale. A noncommunicating being's condition can constrain assessment through other warranted evidence. Disagreement alone neither defeats an account nor demonstrates bias. The right to refuse does not universally depend on articulating a persuasive objection. Nor is accepting every objection a condition of inclusion: the grounds, actual consequences and protected relations need assessment.

If every sign of difficulty is redescribed as resistance to unity, the proposed perspective has insulated its account from the lives it claims to include. If the account recognizes the difficulty and warrants a continued burden because of a real competing consideration, that is a different case. We must preserve it rather than require a favorable outcome as proof of adequate understanding.

The conditional inference is limited but positive: an account claiming to include the relevant lives cannot establish their joint improvement solely through a measure that omits an independently identified consequence necessary to that claim. Further evidence or a narrower claim is required. This applies the inherited identification argument; it is not a new formal theorem or a proof that sufficiently accurate information necessarily produces concern.

### Consequence for goodness and the necessary root

Ren's correction strengthens the proposed generative constitution of goodness. The inquiry can concern how actual patterns of experiencing, relating and responding become sustaining or destructive, including how the distinction between one's own life and another's enters that formation. It need not begin with value owned by one self and ask how it crosses into another sealed domain. Nor must goodness reduce to the greatest output of an encompassing system: that system's success may still misdescribe what it does to its differentiated lives.

The account now has a more faithful target: **warranted development of the activity including both, with the formation of perspectives and purposes inside the explanation**. “Warranted” names work still to do. Benevolent endorsement, wider identification and successful coordination cannot simply define it. The positive patient-ground and legitimate self-direction remain proposals to justify through their actual constitutive relations and substantive rivals.

**Result:** the former fixed-perspective comparison cannot establish an unavoidable opposition between self-direction and responding to another. A widened perspective can change the practical whole, while actual participation can form additional goods. This is a constructive explanatory possibility with correction tests; it is not a universal theorem that non-self entails helping, agreement or a particular distribution.

**Next connection:** identify which transformation of perspective constitutes better understanding or a better way of relating, rather than merely a new identity, while preserving actual consequences for both. The decisive comparison should keep the independently identified condition and response in view while testing whether the wider formation answers them or absorbs them into a preferred story. A substantive rival preserving that whole can revise the claimed ethical direction. Accepted conditional results remain intact; no new model, empirical run or Lean proof is claimed.


## 53. Warranted attention within a shared perspective

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **formed perspective → warranted understanding of shared conditions → revision of purposes and ways of relating**. The preceding study changed the comparison in response to [Ren's non-self correction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-non-self-and-a-perspective-including-both.md). This study asks what makes that transformation an improvement. It preserves the completed conditional foundation and the stronger unfinished necessity objective. The inherited proof-receipt mismatch is a separate preservation limitation, not philosophical evidence or a reason to rerun unrelated science.

### What the earlier correction makes possible

The helper's life is formed within the same activity as the lives it affects. A perspective including both can reveal dependencies through which another's wellbeing sustains the helper's present world, forms future capacities, or partly constitutes a worthwhile relationship. The [new shared-world account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#others-wellbeing-and-the-world-in-which-we-continue) positively develops this route through already warranted stakes and identified dependencies. Concern need not first pass through a universal stranger-relief argument. Neither must the helper remain an unchanged beneficiary waiting for a later return.

This gives a concrete answer to the suggestion that helping changes the shape of a life. Such change can be the formation of something worthwhile within that life and relationship. Whether it is worthwhile depends on what develops and what it does to the differentiated participants. Calling the development shared does not settle that question. The helper's exhaustion, the other's agency, valid commitments and unavailable alternatives remain within the account.

Three questions therefore remain related but distinguishable: whether understanding has become more adequate; whether experience or the relationship has actually changed; and whether that change is justified. A single interaction can do all three. Their analytical separation preserves the possibility of mistaken belief, actual relief under a mistaken explanation, and justified understanding without a new opportunity to act. It does not assign mind, world and value independent foundations.

### Attention is itself activity

[Murdoch's primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#50-warranted-attention-within-a-shared-perspective) permits moral improvement with unchanged outward conduct, while explicitly allowing self-deception and acknowledging its normative premises. This corrects any reading of §52 that makes a new public output necessary for better understanding.

Our generative reconstruction can treat a changed way of understanding another as actual activity within a history. It can undo an unfair interpretation, alter what is salient, or change an experience before a different intervention is possible. A person absent from the present encounter can still be understood more faithfully through warranted attention to their history. This is a proposal about what the account must be able to explain, not evidence that every private conviction is accurate or valuable.

Warrant need not take the form of an immediately observable behavioral difference. Nevertheless, claiming insight does not put it beyond criticism. Relevant particulars, context, alternative interpretations and the limits of available evidence still matter. The practical tests below apply to claims about effective response or a relationship's organization; they are not exhaustive detectors of inner moral improvement.

### A positive comparison that preserves real helping

Consider a shared practice in which an experienced participant helps another develop an activity. Both benefit overall, and participating genuinely changes the helper's life. At first, taking over a difficult part enables participation. Later, the same intervention can interrupt a capacity that has developed. The earlier observations need not have been false; the relation between intervention and undertaking has changed.

Fix the comparison before judging it. Suppose the capacity's value, the shared undertaking and the relevant permissions are independently warranted. Evidence supports a feasible continuation that preserves the needed assistance while allowing the capacity to operate. The helper's real commitments and costs remain represented. Under these conditions, reconsidering the intervention has a positive ground: it can better realize the good already at issue. This practical inference uses that defended good, the changed dependency and the available continuation. It does not extract ethical direction from bare causal connection.

Now compare a responder who accurately understands both lives, brings real benefit and develops a sincere caring identity, but preserves control over how the relationship may change. This is a stronger rival than an uninformed projector. Their causal and psychological knowledge can improve while the other's significance remains admitted only within a preferred arrangement. A purpose-sensitive response instead allows warranted evidence to challenge that arrangement, including the helper's desired centrality.

The distinction does not make reduced dependence or relinquishing a role automatically better. Continued dependence can be appropriate; withdrawal can abandon a real responsibility. The outcome may warrant maintaining, modifying or ending an intervention. Nor does the accurate controller stipulate complete moral understanding and then arbitrarily lose its consequences: it preserves accurate causal and psychological understanding, whose sufficiency is being tested. A richer account of just understanding must explain the additional constitutive relation it claims.

The comparison also cannot infer a hidden motive from identical observable conduct. Where the available histories do not discriminate the two accounts, their identification remains unresolved. This is a proposed philosophical test, not an executed study or an instrument for diagnosing people.

### What warrants the changed understanding

The constructive proposal is **attention whose interpretation remains answerable to the actual lives and shared conditions it purports to understand, including evidence that can revise its purposes**. This states a candidate relation to investigate, rather than a synonym for benevolent feeling. A bounded inquiry can proceed as follows:

1. Specify the earlier interpretation and a serious alternative. For example, apparent resistance may concern an imposed role, a genuine difficulty, or a justified competing commitment. No alternative becomes true merely because it is kinder.
2. Identify the particulars that support revision and what would count against it. The other's account can matter without becoming infallible; inability to communicate does not eliminate other warranted evidence.
3. Distinguish discovery from formation. Learning that an activity was already distressing differs from changing that experience through attention or interaction. Both can matter, but they answer different claims.
4. Where better practical relating is claimed, examine whether warranted consequences can change the purpose or arrangement itself. Evidence should not merely refine compliance with a goal whose relevance is the disputed issue.

These conditions do not supply a complete moral criterion. They identify why a particular transformation can improve understanding and realization of a defended good, and how it can fail. The normative work includes defending that good, the relevant claims and the force of the practical inference. Independent disagreement about these grounds remains possible. A generated endorsement cannot certify itself simply because the new identity embraces it.

The [existing perspective-identification dossier](HUIGEN-HARM-IDENTIFICATION.md) supplies a bounded empirical caution about imagining another's view and obtaining evidence from them. Its previously inspected study concerns prediction accuracy; it neither measures all moral understanding nor establishes this proposed philosophical account. No new empirical reading or reanalysis is added here.

### The mechanism interface already achieved

The accepted [acquired-applicability construction](../../2026-10-05-generative-health/succession/ACQUIRED-APPLICABILITY-INTEGRATION.md) gives a relevant conditional realization. Its sensor model remains correct while a use fails. The acquired consequence retains two possibilities—complemented application or absent transfer—and guides a discriminating next encounter. A matched reader clamp retains the same record but repeats the ineffective route. Misleading feedback can instead preserve a false interpretation and stop inquiry.

This is an inherited result, not a new ethical simulation. It shows one way consequences can change the application of retained knowledge without first falsifying that knowledge. Failure also does not establish the opposite application. The model supplies the candidate family, contact calibration, inquiry rule and productive undertaking; it does not derive a person's good or legitimate authority. Its native join is already completed at the declared finite scope and should not be reopened by this philosophical comparison.

Our stronger question concerns what warrants revising the undertaking or its admissible alternatives when several real goods and claims are involved. A mechanism for acquired correction helps explain realization; it does not by itself settle this question. Conversely, a defensible practical revision is not unreal merely because the present mechanism lacks its representation.

### Goodness, generativity and scientific containment

The proposed goodness concerns what the unfolding realizes for the lives within it. Sustaining capacities, relieving an actual burden, understanding someone fairly and forming worthwhile relations are candidate goods to explain and justify. Creating more activity, stronger identification or more effective control does not suffice: the accurate controller can produce all three. This preserves Ren's generative approach while distinguishing an account of how goodness forms from declaring every formation good.

Murdoch also contests science's exhaustive containment of moral understanding. Our response is an explanatory obligation: including attention's causal realization in a generative account is weaker than explaining its warranted character. The latter cannot be secured by stipulating that scientific adequacy already includes the desired ethics.

A shared generative foundation can investigate historical understanding, its realization and its standards within one unfolding. Doing so need not reduce meaning to publicly changed outputs or posit a separate moral substance. But this possibility is not yet proof that every scientific philosophy must accept the entire theory. Compatibility with well-supported science, explanation of moral development, and necessity of this particular foundation remain distinct claims. Evidence against a claimed dependency must be allowed to revise the account rather than be excluded by redefining real science.

**Result:** the widened perspective gains a positive, testable philosophical content: it can disclose and help realize shared goods by correcting understanding and the application of purposes, while preserving differentiated lives. Better attention can matter before changed outward conduct. Accurate benefit-producing control remains a substantive rival where a claimed shared practice prevents warranted revision of its own organization.

**Next connection:** justify a concrete revision of a shared undertaking when the other's developing capacity, the helper's meaningful commitment and a valid existing claim pull in different directions. Identify which dependencies and grounds support maintaining, changing or relinquishing the role, without freezing prior preferences or treating a new preference as its own warrant. The shared-world route remains available; a universal stranger-relief proof is not its entry condition. No new model, empirical run or Lean result is claimed, and the stronger whole-root necessity remains open.


## 54. Successful support and continuing commitments

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **shared support → developed capacity → changed fulfillment or justified amendment of a continuing undertaking**. The preceding turn made progress; at resumption all 30 artifact hashes matched the partial 823-link/492-anchor receipt, and 741 worktree entries were present. This study preserves the [nondual method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md), the accepted conditional foundation and the stronger unfinished whole-root objective. The inherited Depletion receipt mismatch is separately retained; no scientific rerun is warranted by this philosophical extension.

### The new connection is generated by success

Section 49 already distinguished revising an end, authority to revise it and consequences that survive its abandonment. Section 53 investigated warranted attention and purpose-sensitive correction. The [current shared-world account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#shared-stakes-actual-contribution-and-usable-correction) now supplies a conditional dominance comparison and a positive reason for preserving usable corrective capacities. Those are inherited advances, not results introduced here.

The present question is different: **can the success of a shared practice change what faithful continuation requires?** Helping can develop a capacity whose former absence justified a particular mode of help. Its appearance can remove that rationale without making the earlier response mistaken. The development can also change the helper's identity and the relationship's possibilities. This is not merely an external event disturbing an otherwise fixed system; the practice itself has helped form its new conditions.

Begin with that history. Differentiated participants, their skills, expectations and responsibilities form through activity. One person may initially perform a part that another cannot yet perform. Later, warranted evidence of developed capacity can support enabling, sharing or standing by. The same continuing purpose may call for a different mode. Conversely, joint performance may itself constitute a valued activity, or be the content of a valid undertaking. The theory must explain these distinctions within the relationship, rather than identify care with either permanent intervention or inevitable withdrawal.

### Hold the comparison fixed before changing the verdict

Consider two competent adults engaged in a musical practice. Earlier assistance contributes to a new capacity. They now have the same relevant abilities and physically feasible performance options in the two cases below. The helper has a real interest in continuing their joint activity; the other's independent performance has a real developmental attraction. Assume the undertakings specified in each case are legitimate within their stated limits. That validity is a defended input to this comparison, not a consequence of somebody saying that they agreed.

| Actual history of the undertaking | What the new capacity changes | What it does not settle |
|---|---|---|
| They undertake that the experienced participant will support the other's participation as needed through the preparation period, with the mode adjusted to developed ability. | Taking over a part may cease to be needed. Enabling or standing by may perform the same undertaking on its current terms. | Availability through the agreed period may still be owed. The helper's interest in joint activity has not disappeared. |
| They also validly undertake a particular joint performance at a specified event. | The other's new ability defeats the claim that a joint performance is the only feasible way to participate. | A solo performance does not thereby fulfill the particular joint undertaking. Its amendment, release or an independently warranted exception remains a separate question. |

The histories differ in an actual formation of practical relations. They are not an unchanged complete situation with a freely toggled obligation. Nor are every present fact and record declared identical: only the listed ability and feasibility features are matched. Actual words, context, shared plans, permitted variations and reliance bear on what was undertaken. Neither an observed pattern nor a preferred endpoint alone identifies its full content.

The example is conceptual, not an empirical finding. It holds open a genuine conflict. The joint event can prevent a desired opportunity for independent performance; a solo event can remove a meaningful shared activity and violate an outstanding claim. Do not invent an equally good second event, willing substitute or costless release to make every consideration align. A bounded, valid commitment can sometimes justify retaining a mode despite an opportunity forgone. That conclusion requires the relevant comparison of grounds; it does not follow merely from the label commitment.

### Three different forms of permissible change

These distinguish justificatory questions, not mutually exclusive classes of action. One adjustment can both fulfill unchanged terms and exercise an already granted discretion.

First, **changed fulfillment** can occur under unchanged content. If the undertaking is adaptive support, its faithful realization need not repeat yesterday's action. A practice that continues taking over merely because it once did so may cease to realize the support it undertook. This is a positive reason for appropriate adjustment grounded in the continuing undertaking and its actual conditions. It need not discharge the undertaking or require renegotiating it.

Second, **authorized revision** can use a power already established within the shared arrangement. An agreement may allow one participant to choose a different mode under identified conditions. Exercising that power within its warranted limits need not await fresh concurrence. The scope of the power matters: discretion over a method does not automatically include authority to withdraw the promised activity, impose a new burden or release another person's independent claim.

Third, **amendment** changes what the participants are committed to doing. A proposed solo substitute for a specifically promised joint event may belong here. An adequate account must identify who can agree to the change, what remains outstanding, and whether the new arrangement is itself legitimate. Mutual agreement cannot by itself discharge a third party's claim. Refusal to amend can also require appraisal; having standing to insist is not a complete all-things justification for insisting in every circumstance.

The [new Gilbert comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#51-successful-support-and-continuing-commitments) identifies advance authorization and incompletely specified shared plans as resources for explaining such development. Its account of commitment-based obligation does not itself establish moral validity. We use this distinction without adopting every shared intention as a morally binding promise or treating a collective as one experiencing subject.

Stable commitments can therefore support the formation of new arrangements. Participants need not specify every detail in advance if an adequately grounded continuing undertaking supplies a framework within which they can negotiate. But stability alone does not make that framework good: it can also secure an objectionable arrangement. The explanation must retain the purposes, protections and actual consequences through which this particular framework is defended.

### Selective revision preserves the sources of reasons

Let the earlier justification for a mode include the premise that a relevant part cannot presently be undertaken otherwise. Successful support can make that premise false. The inference to the old mode from that premise no longer succeeds. This does not establish that the mode has no other justification. A particular promise, accrued reliance, a shared good or the helper's meaningful participation may remain relevant.

Equally, maintaining a valid undertaking does not preserve every earlier means conclusion. In the adaptive case, changed conduct may be required precisely because the undertaking continues. An account that identifies commitment with an invariant action list cannot capture this. An account that identifies fulfillment with whatever outcome is now preferred cannot capture the particular-performance case.

The [existing claim-persistence model, T9](HUIGEN-REASON-TRANSPORT.md#12-assurance-can-create-a-claim-that-outlasts-inquiry), already distinguishes legitimate creation, continuing activation and actual discharge. It neither creates validity by recording a claim nor settles priority between claims. Whether a fixed activation condition holds can change while the claim remains; valid release can instead change what is outstanding. Unchanged terms can also call for a different mode of performance, where their independently warranted interpretation supports that application. An authorized change of the terms requires its own justified operation; T9 does not implement amendment of its stored claim content. No new theorem is needed for the inherited bookkeeping, and this study introduces none.

The useful conclusion is a constraint on a justified recommendation. Specify which ground has ceased, which is newly applicable, which is fulfilled in a different way and which still supports a competing continuation. These cannot be replaced with a general assertion that development is good or that commitments must be honored unchanged. Where the grounds do not determine a unique choice, the result should preserve the unresolved conflict rather than manufacture a ranking.

### What the shared perspective adds

[Ren's perspective including both](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-non-self-and-a-perspective-including-both.md) includes the formation and transformation of the helper's life. It also includes the real practical relations formed between the participants. Their new capacities need not be treated as an attack on an antecedently finished self. Their earlier joint activity need not be treated as disposable merely because a new possibility appears.

The helper's meaningful involvement can be a genuine consideration without automatically becoming an entitlement to continued dependence. The other's development can be good without automatically discharging a particular undertaking. Non-self therefore allows differentiated continuity: capacities, purposes and modes can change while some relations remain, and legitimate amendment can transform those relations as well. The explanation does not need permanent owners behind these processes, but it must retain whose participation and consequences make the claim intelligible.

This also refines good as generative. Worthwhile support can generate capacities that change how support should continue. Its goodness need not be measured by perpetuating the helper's role, multiplying interventions or maximizing future flexibility. Present enjoyment, completion and a bounded shared performance can matter too. A general theory must explain both justified development and justified stability through the actual grounds, including cases where they compete.

### Consequence for the necessary-foundation claim

A theory claiming to explain these shared practices must preserve the distinction between changing a realization and changing what is undertaken. The paired comparison provides a substantive target for that requirement. An adequate representation may compress the history into warranted current content and powers; it need not reproduce every prior event. But if it collapses the two relevant histories into one success score, that score alone cannot justify their different commitment judgments.

This is a conditional requirement of adequacy for the stated phenomenon. It supports the generative account's attention to formation, changing application and continuing consequences. It does not yet establish that every scientific philosophy must adopt this entire theory or Gilbert's account. Competing theories can be tested by whether they preserve and explain the same distinctions, rather than by whether they use our terminology. Original conditional foundation acceptance remains closed at its own scope.

**Result:** the success of concern can generate grounds for changing its form. Sometimes that change fulfills a continuing commitment; sometimes it exercises an already granted power; sometimes it requires amendment of what is owed. The account now connects development to this three-way distinction while preserving real conflict and independent grounds. A valid specific-performance condition, a failed efficacy claim or a justified exception can change the recommendation.

**Next connection:** investigate when a proposal to renegotiate has a positive ground beyond permission to ask, and what can justify accepting or refusing it when both the existing shared activity and the emerging capacity matter. Identify the actual burdens of transition, continuing support and powers of each participant. A successful account would explain how the relationship can rightly change without making prior authorization permanent control or present preference a universal release. No new model, empirical run, formal proof or universal ethical derivation is claimed.


## 55. Reasons to revise and powers to release

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **formed commitments → reasons governing the exercise of revision powers → changed conditions of further activity**. The preceding round made progress. At resumption all 30 artifact hashes matched; 756 worktree entries were present. This closes the present commitment comparison at a bounded philosophical scope and returns its remaining dependency to the necessary-foundation investigation. It preserves the [nondual method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md), the accepted conditional foundation and the stronger unfinished objective. The inherited proof-receipt mismatch remains a separate limitation.

### Include the exercise of authority in the shared perspective

Section 54 established that successful support can change an appropriate mode without automatically changing an undertaking. It left the positive ground for renegotiation open. The further question concerns the participant who can legitimately change that undertaking: **how should that power be exercised as the relationship develops?** Their present authority is itself a formed relation that changes what the other can permissibly do. It belongs inside the practical comparison.

[Ren's correction about goodness without return](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-goodness-without-return-to-self.md), now integrated into [the governing account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#goodness-beyond-return-to-an-individual), prevents a mistaken entry condition: release need not first be shown to benefit the person releasing the claim. A continuation can realize good elsewhere while ending an activity they reasonably value. That loss remains a consideration; it does not make the further good invisible. Present enjoyment and relief also need no later product to count.

Reconstruct the shared history before isolating these roles. Earlier assistance forms capacity, trust and particular undertakings. Later development changes the possibilities that those relations support or restrict. The helper's changed life is part of this unfolding. Describing a claimant, performer and beneficiary preserves differences in authority and consequence; treating them as antecedently complete, unrelated selves would omit the formation being explained. Conversely, describing a shared process cannot authorize one participant to speak for both.

### A concrete constraint, with a genuine limiting case

The [new primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#52-reasons-to-revise-and-powers-to-release) provides a conditional means–end constraint on the claimholder's practical stance. Apply it only where the holder actually intends the promised performance, knows that a particular enabling contribution is indispensable, and controls that contribution within the undertaking's actual allocation of responsibilities. Refusing every timely way to enable it while retaining that intention calls for repairing the plan. Providing support, changing the arrangement or validly releasing it can be different responses. Mere delay before the contribution is needed does not establish incoherence.

This is not a free duty to fund another person's performance. Nor does possession of a right always imply the present intention specified above. An option can be retained without intending its exercise. Changing one's mind may repair one's intention structure, but does not, without an appropriate normative act or condition, establish that another person's undertaking has been discharged. A promisee's unilateral release power must also be distinguished from a joint undertaking that requires concurrence; the actual power must be identified, not inferred from generic talk of commitment.

Retain the strongest limiting case from the preceding comparison. The helper values a particular joint performance, can supply its necessary support and coherently intends to do so. The other's new capacity makes solo performance possible and attractive. **Capacity growth alone creates no inconsistency in continuing the joint undertaking.** The case may still favor amendment for substantive reasons, but the new coherence argument does not decide it. This preserves the actual conflict rather than inventing failure to enable in order to obtain release.

### The positive argument concerns further good

Our proposed inference is conditional on the relational ethical ground already under investigation:

1. There is independently warranted good in a proposed continuation, assessed through actual experience, capacities and affected conditions, rather than merely the initiator's preference or the amount of activity produced.
2. A legitimate, bounded exercise of this participant's revision power would enable that good; the causal and normative connection is established in this case.
3. The proposal, transition and eventual action are assessed with their burdens, permissions, reliance and independent claims intact.

Under the defended premise that this good is a practical reason for an eligible participant, these conditions provide a contributory reason to consider and potentially grant revision. A valid release can make room for a developing activity and give those involved settled permission to proceed. That is a particular way of enabling further good. It need not repay the releaser or perpetuate their role. It also need not dominate every alternative: an all-things judgment still has to address the real lost collaboration and any protected discretion.

Four moments must therefore remain distinct: proposing a change, having reason to accept it, validly making it, and acting under the resulting arrangement. A reason at the second moment does not authorize skipping the third. Conversely, a performer's lack of unilateral authority does not establish that the holder has no reason to grant release. An independently justified exception can warrant nonperformance without changing what the original commitment was; that is another question, not a fictitious release.

The [Owens counterweight](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#52-reasons-to-revise-and-powers-to-release) makes the crucial challenge substantive: a refusal can be unreasonable while release is not obligatory, and a claim can remain. We retain that rival instead of defining adequate judgment as agreement with release. To establish a duty here, the generative account must defend how this particular use of authority should respond to the affected good. Neither causal connection nor coherence alone completes that argument.

### What has changed in the derivation

The dependency difference from §54 is precise. There the question was which undertaking and powers persist as capacities develop. Here their exercise becomes an object of justified practical judgment. This advances **H10**, answerability within formed practical relations, and **H11**, context-sensitive wisdom, under stated grounds. The plan constraint supplies one concrete repair reason. The release argument shows how an existing power can participate in further good, including beyond its holder.

It does not discharge **H9**, the grounding and scope of noninstrumental reasons, or **H12**, necessity of the whole proposed root. The relevance of independently warranted good, the legitimacy of the undertaking and power, and the comparative judgment remain substantive dependencies. Locating them within generative reality does not make them external impositions; recording them as supplied to this argument does not prove them irreducible. Their connected explanation remains the central task.

The [existing T9 interface](HUIGEN-REASON-TRANSPORT.md#12-assurance-can-create-a-claim-that-outlasts-inquiry) can track legitimate discharge once its conditions are provided. It does not derive reasons for discharging a claim or implement amendment. Existing native succession results retain an explicit normative ground. No new controller or theorem is claimed, and no scientific rerun follows from this extension.

### Close this comparison and return to the root claim

The local commitment branch now has a bounded result: successful support can change fulfillment; some changes require the exercise of a formed power; that exercise can itself be assessed for how it enables or obstructs warranted further good. Legitimate stability, particular shared activities and unresolved conflicts remain. This is a philosophical reconstruction with live premises, not a completed universal ethics.

For a rival account explaining this target, the test is whether it preserves the relevant differences among ability, undertaking, authority, exercise and consequence. Another vocabulary or ontology may do so. A scientific philosophy need not adopt our entire formulation merely to pass this local test. The stronger claim requires an independently specified class of inquiry, a necessary root for that class and warranted coverage of the substantive ethical conclusions. Valid consequence transmits whatever necessity has been established; it does not establish the starting basis by itself.

**Next central connection:** test the normative relevance of actual good and its onward conditions without requiring return to an initiating self. Compare accounts that grant the actual shared history and good but explain their practical authority differently. Identify which relation the generative account explains indispensably, which a rival also preserves and what evidence or argument could overturn the proposed connection. This returns directly to H9/H12 rather than extending the sequence with another isolated promise case. It also retains Ren's question: calling goodness generative must explain ethical direction, not identify all production with goodness or hide the answer in a preferred success measure.


## 56. Scientific disagreement and practical interpretation

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **actual good beyond return to self → practical significance → necessity across scientific interpretations**. The previous turn made progress. At resumption all 30 recorded artifact hashes matched the partial 848-link/512-anchor receipt; 772 worktree entries were present. This returns directly to H9/H12 after the bounded commitment comparison. The [nondual method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md) and [no-return account](GOODNESS-WITHOUT-RETURN-INTEGRATION.md) govern the reconstruction. No new scientific run is claimed.

### A stronger rival must retain the practical phenomenon

A philosophy that explains how concern develops is not thereby committed to every moral judgment made by the people it explains. Conversely, a philosophy that interprets moral discourse differently may affirm the very concern we defend. The necessity inquiry must identify which of these situations obtains before treating a rival as either a counterexample or an inadequate philosophy.

The [mature Carnap comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#53-scientific-disagreement-and-practical-interpretation) is useful because it retains reasoning about concern and permits analytical distinctions within one situation. Neither nonduality nor lack of private return dismisses it. Its alternative interpretation must be assessed at the level of what particular judgments mean and what supports them. The reply is a substantive proposal, not a completed proof that every relevant normative account can be replaced.

Our target is the existing claim that an actual good can favor an eligible response without benefiting the responder. A rival that endorses this practical judgment but disputes its metaphysical explanation is not yet denying that target. A rival allowing different all-things choices also need not deny it: the current theory preserves conflicting reasons and does not promise one universally determined choice. The whole-root argument must not acquire stronger requirements merely to manufacture opposition.

### Three differences that must not be conflated

| Possible disagreement | What must be established before drawing a conclusion |
|---|---|
| Whether a response has any favor from the affected good | Identify the same good, available response, practical addressee and conditions. This is a direct dispute over the proposed reason. |
| Whether that favor decisively settles the response | Preserve competing claims, costs and permissions. A different final judgment can coexist with the same contributory reason. |
| How the reason judgment is interpreted or grounded | Determine whether the rival preserves its practical force and warranted uses, or replaces it with a report about endorsement. Different metatheory alone is not proof of different practical content. |

These are distinctions in the investigation, not independent substances placed outside generative unfolding. Beliefs, commitments, experiences and actions may form together while playing different roles in inference. A shared origin does not, on its own, establish that one assertion logically entails every practical commitment associated with it. Equally, separate notation does not refute a warranted constitutive connection.

The source's disagreement example matches beliefs, not the entire actual history and organization of the participants. It therefore cannot show that the very same complete generative situation supports arbitrarily reassigned normative truths. Nor does variation in final choices refute a baseline reason. The source leaves factual value interpretations available and its full account of mixed inference unfinished; our response must address the actual disputed content rather than substitute a blanket denial of ethics.

### Reconstruct one application in both accounts

Keep the existing no-return application fixed: an available, permissible response would realize an independently identified good in an affected life, and the relevant competing consequences have been considered. The response supplies no advantage to its initiator. This is an argument template, not a claim that these conditions obtain in every real case.

The generative proposal says that the actual transformation, together with the eligible relation to it, constitutes a practical consideration. Evidence about the transformation and the response's means supplies the application. A rival may instead express a general practical commitment reaching affected lives, combine it with the same evidence and endorse the same response. This is our reconstruction of a possible comparison, not a quotation or a fully executed translation of the rival's philosophy.

Both can preserve the good beyond its initiator. What remains to test is **why the general practical consideration is warranted and what kind of judgment expresses it**. The first account must defend the constitutive relation; the second must explain its commitment's justificatory status and its rational revision. Agreement in conduct supplies neither defense. Disagreement about semantics supplies neither refutation.

The strongest test asks what happens when an otherwise competent inquirer declines the general practical commitment. The theory claims that a relevant reason can remain despite that refusal. An expressivist might itself condemn the refusal categorically; expressivism alone does not decide the practical question. To establish scientific necessity, we must show which independently justified condition of the specified inquiry the refusal violates, or establish the reason by another necessary argument that the inquiry must respect. To establish a counterexample, a rival must defend its refusal while retaining that target. Neither side may obtain its conclusion by defining scientific adequacy to include it.

This identifies a real remaining explanation, rather than a proof that value is external to generation. The history can contain the formation of practical standards and their correction. The unresolved issue is the validity and scope of the particular standard, not its residence in a second world.

### Preserve force as well as reference

The [new whole-theory interpretation audit](HUIGEN-FULL-THEORY-AUDIT.md#10-necessary-practical-content-across-scientific-interpretations) turns this into a specific requirement. A faithful interpretation must preserve what a judgment concerns, its practical kind, its local premises and the relevant inferential relations. Translating a reason into the fact that someone happens to approve does not by itself meet that requirement. Nor does matching a policy's outputs while erasing permissions or continuing claims.

Admitting a rival to comparison must not already require its endorsement of H9. Representing the disputed judgment and assessing its warranted standing are separate tasks. One coherent interpretation must cover the jointly asserted package; changing the meaning of good for each troublesome conclusion would evade the whole-root test. This extends the existing interpretation discipline to the actual moral dispute without claiming that every alternative semantics has passed it.

The current Lean closure is explicitly a truth-valued fragment. It does not automatically establish a consequence relation for pure practical expressions. A faithful account could preserve the same practical inferences across different metatheories, but the translation and mixed rules would need justification. Calling a practical commitment accepted cannot silently replace calling it warranted. The existing formal theorem and its historical verification retain their scopes and inherited receipt limitation.

### Result and next central connection

The new result narrows the alleged disagreement while strengthening the test of necessity. A humane, nondual-compatible scientific philosophy is a serious comparison even when it disputes our normative explanation. It is not a verified counterexample merely because its language differs. The foundation must earn the indispensable practical content and a faithful account of its generation; it need not prove that every different metatheory rejects that content.

H9's ground and address remain open. H12 now has a more precise coverage obligation for practical judgments; truth preservation in the existing fragment cannot stand in for it. Conditional foundation acceptance is unchanged. The comparison adds no empirical finding, new controller or formal proof.

**Next central connection:** specify and test one rule carrying an independently warranted actual-good judgment into a practical reason under eligible control, using the same target and local conditions across competing interpretations. Establish what justifies the rule and whether the rival can preserve its practical force; keep mere acceptance, unique choice and unconditional truth distinct. This is the direct normative dependency, not another search for private reward or a further isolated helping scenario.


## 57. Whose acceptance matters to a practical reason?

6 October 2026. AI-authored execution of the preceding interpretation test through **actual relief and present access → contributory moral favor → appraisal of the principle supporting that inference**. The previous round made progress. At resumption all 30 recorded artifact hashes matched the partial 863-link/524-anchor receipt; 794 worktree entries were present. The [nondual method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md), the [relational reason argument](RELATION-AND-REASON-ARGUMENT.md) and the no-return direction govern this comparison. No new publication, formal proof or scientific run is claimed.

### Fix the target before comparing the two applications

The target is an objective contributory moral reason: an actual relief can count in favor of this actor's response for the affected being's sake. This is different from a duty, a unique best choice or unrestricted rational authority. The stronger programme retains those additional questions where claimed; this local test does not acquire them by changing the meaning of moral favor.

Begin within the shared activity. A particular being undergoes a severe, still-unwanted adverse episode. Another participant has a known response that would relieve that episode while preserving the experiencing continuity. A judging participant considers the response. Those roles can overlap in an actual history. Naming them separately here identifies whose undergoing, action and judgment are at issue; it does not introduce three independently constituted substances.

Call the judging participant A, the responding participant B and the affected being C. Fix three application premises:

- **F1:** the identified condition is C's actual adverse undergoing; relief would genuinely improve that undergoing.
- **F2:** the specified response available to B would produce that relief through the identified means.
- **F3:** B has the relevant control and independently warranted permission. The decision's actual claims, restrictions and competing consequences have been assessed at the declared scope.

F1 contains the defended patient-good claim, not merely a sensor reading. F3 cannot mean that the response is already morally favored. Permission and a justified restriction on the basis of choice need their own identification. In the ordinary comparison, no prior promise, causation of the burden, adopted helping project or private return is supplied. These are explicit scope conditions, not a claim that every real encounter lacks such relations.

### Work through the inference under both interpretations

| Role in the application | Generative constitutive reconstruction | Practical-principle reconstruction |
|---|---|---|
| General ground | **G:** the actual good and B's present responsive relation constitute a contributory moral consideration under the stated conditions. | **P:** judge cases with those same good, access and permission conditions as having that contributory favor for the relevant actor's response. |
| Application | F1–F3 identify a case of the defended constitutive relationship. | A applies the general practical principle to F1–F3. |
| Practical content | This actual relief counts in favor of B's response, whether B has adopted a helping purpose or not. | A endorses that same actor-directed favor, without including B's prior endorsement in P's conditions. |
| Warrant still to establish | Why G is a true or otherwise warranted account of the practical relation, and how far it applies. | Why P is warranted, and whether this interpretation preserves the full practical authority claimed rather than only an endorsement. |

The second column is a reconstruction of the current generative proposal. The third is our deliberately weaker adaptation of the [primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#54-whose-acceptance-matters-to-a-practical-reason), not a completed translation of the whole theory. The source's own example has small actor benefits and concludes comparative moral better/ought. We have expressly supplied a no-return, pro tanto principle for the present test. Neither the altered principle nor its validity is attributed to the source.

The inference has a real common feature: **neither application uses B's acceptance of P or B's desire to help as a premise**. The practical-principle route can therefore judge acquiring concern favorably before B does so. It need not treat the acquisition as a private event that first makes C's relief worthy of consideration. This removes an alleged motivational contrast between the two interpretations; it does not establish their complete semantic equivalence or common stance-independent authority.

### Keep three acts of acceptance separate

The [targeted primary rereading](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#54-whose-acceptance-matters-to-a-practical-reason) distinguishes A's practical premise, B's conduct and an assessor's appraisal of A's reasoning. Our analysis draws the following consequence.

B's indifference concerns motivation or uptake. A's acceptance of P makes P available in A's deliberation. An assessor may recognize the structure of that application while disputing P's warrant. Moving from one role to another changes the question: a premise accepted by the judge is not thereby an attitude the actor must already hold, and accepted premises are not automatically justified premises.

An objective moral claim about B can therefore be directed to B despite indifference under the proposed interpretation. Whether that claim is objectively correct remains open to justification. Conversely, A's later rejection of P would withdraw this route in A's deliberation; it would not by itself establish that the reason disappeared from the situation. A's judgment may also causally change the situation if it becomes a promise, threat, intervention or public commitment. Such effects must be included when present; the narrow acceptance comparison stipulates none of them.

This distinction directly answers the earlier concern that wanting good reasons cannot establish what they are. A willingness to reason, an adopted norm and a sound application of that norm are different achievements. An immanent account can explain how all three form and interact without identifying their causal occurrence with their justificatory success.

### Corrections that the worked comparison can actually distinguish

| Change being tested | Consequence at this declared scope |
|---|---|
| The response suppresses a report but does not relieve the actual episode. | F2 fails; this particular relief application loses its basis even if A and B approve. A different benefit would need its own account. |
| Reliable application evidence is lost, while the actual relief relation may remain. | Warranted confidence and responsible choice change. Loss of evidence does not establish that the actual good or reason ceased. |
| B privately ceases wanting to help; control, permission, relevant commitments and the actual response relation remain as specified. | Neither displayed inference contains a premise defeated by that change alone. A rival requiring B's endorsement must defend that additional restriction. |
| A legitimately established decision rule excludes this consideration as a basis for this particular choice. | The application requires a scope qualification. Actual benefit and permission of the resulting outcome need not authorize that basis of selection. |
| A ceases endorsing P while the specified practical situation remains. | This derivation is no longer A's accepted route. That is not a demonstration that G is false or that all warranted routes have ended. |

The exclusion case is a materially different normative history. The inherited merit-only award example tests a stronger rule that would infer a valid selection reason from benefit and a permissible outcome alone. It does not refute the clean stranger comparison by inserting an institution that was absent there. Keep the same decision fixed when assessing exclusion; possible concern through timing or another action does not restore the excluded basis for that verdict. This preserves a genuine counterexample without allowing private indifference to imitate legitimate authority.

### What supports the positive ground, and what remains unresolved

The inherited positive argument concerns what makes concern worth forming and its means worth correcting: an actual burden can be relieved, and an ineffective gesture lacks that point even when equally satisfying to the helper. The two reconstructed applications can both preserve these judgments. P was constructed to preserve G's application judgments, so their agreement is not independent corroboration of those judgments. Nor does a shared response establish that either ultimate account is correct. The dependency clarification in §58 explains how they can belong to one account.

The remaining questions concern G's constitutive explanation and P's warranted authority; the displayed G and P do not yet constitute two conflicting metatheories. Scientific evidence can identify the episode, response and actual consequences; evidence of formation and correction can constrain proposed realizations. This comparison does not show that such evidence alone selects a complete metatheory. Neither does overlap in the applications prove permanent empirical indistinguishability. No complete equivalence or impossibility result has been established.

A successful generative derivation could explain why this relational transformation is a reason, rather than merely stipulating it as a primitive. A successful rival could preserve its practical content through another warranted account. A genuine rejection must address the actual contributory favor under the fixed conditions. Absence of B's helping aim, A's unendorsed premise or a disagreement about final choice is not, by itself, that rejection.

**Result:** the worked application removes a mistaken change of addressee from the necessity dispute. Objective moral address is not the same issue as prior motivation, while an evaluator's acceptance does not certify a principle. H9's substantive warrant and H12's joint coverage remain open. The [central derivation update](HUIGEN-FULL-THEORY-AUDIT.md#11-practical-application-without-the-actors-prior-endorsement) records this precise gain. Accepted conditional completion remains unchanged.

**Next central connection:** compare the warrant for G and P themselves, using the positive prospective-relief argument and any independently justified exclusion under the same moral scope. Determine whether one is a faithful articulation of a shared necessary relationship, whether one omits something the other explains, or whether the practical principle is justified but not established as scientifically indispensable. Do not return to the actor's private motivation as a substitute for that inquiry.


## 58. A ground and its practical articulation

6 October 2026. AI-authored clarification of **warranted practical ground → faithful principle → valid application**, continuing §57. All 30 prior artifact hashes matched the partial receipt at resumption. This corrects the comparison's explanatory structure, rather than claiming another deduction of concern from non-self. The [shared method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md) requires us to explain analytical distinctions within one activity; different explanatory roles alone do not make independent foundations.

Let D identify the actual adverse undergoing, relieving response and eligible access in §57, including its defended patient-good and application conditions. Let R be the same contributory moral favor for this response. G proposes that D constitutes or grounds R. P articulates the corresponding practical principle. These can be parts of one explanation: G addresses why the relation is a reason; P expresses how that reason applies. Our adapted P does not itself deny G. A substantive rival must specify a disagreement about meaning, grounding, warrant or scope; the mere presence of an accepted principle does not do that.

The positive dependency result is conditional but useful. Suppose the actual relation between C's burden and B's permissible relieving response has been warranted as a contributory moral reason. A principle faithfully articulating that same reason can receive its content's warrant from the same ground. It need not introduce an additional independent ethical source. This concerns the justification of the content, not an unsupported inference from a factual description of suffering to a moral conclusion. G's existing substantive defense is retained; this dependency alone neither completes its grounding nor establishes its necessity.

The articulation must preserve the affected being, addressed actor, relevant choice, domain, exceptions and contributory force. A warranted instance supports its corresponding instance; it does not warrant a universal P. A principle that additionally asserts a duty, equal weight or categorical rational authority needs support for those additions. If P is a practical expression rather than a truth-valued proposition, its faithful interpretation and mixed inference remain obligations from §56.
