Neither actual endorsement nor the psychological process of learning the principle follows just from a warranted ground.

The converse also fails as an inference: using P correctly does not establish G as its unique or necessary explanation. P was deliberately constructed to match G's application, so the agreement supplies no independent confirmation of the underlying moral judgments. The inherited [prospective-relief argument](RELATION-AND-REASON-ARGUMENT.md) retains its own substantive defense; expressing its conclusion again does not strengthen that defense. Likewise, the [ownerless comparison](OWNERLESS-ETHICS-SOURCES.md) and [necessity attempt](NECESSITY-ATTEMPT-ARGUMENT.md) already distinguish personal continuity from an independent owner and special concern from exclusive moral scope. No new no-self proof is claimed here.

For **H9**, this removes an artificial demand for two separate ethical foundations while retaining the first actual-good-to-moral-reason obligation. For **H12**, a justified principle can be a faithful articulation of the root's consequence instead of another supplied root premise. This is a hand dependency clarification, not a newly checked extension of the existing formal closure result to practical language. The [central derivation](HUIGEN-FULL-THEORY-AUDIT.md#11-practical-application-without-the-actors-prior-endorsement) now records it.

**Next central connection:** assess the prospective moral favor and the necessity of its proposed explanation separately. An independently adequate rival preserving that favor and its correction conditions while rejecting G's particular constitutive explanation would challenge explanatory necessity. A warranted case satisfying G's stated conditions but excluding that same moral favor would instead challenge G's sufficiency. Neither is supplied by a free predicate change, the actor's indifference, a permission to refuse, or the present matched P. Keep the helper's actual history and legitimate claims in either comparison. Original conditional acceptance is unchanged; the whole-root objective remains open.


## 59. Concrete reasons and their constitutive explanation

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **actual reason-making circumstances → their normative significance → necessity of the proposed constitutive explanation**. The preceding round made progress; all 30 recorded artifact hashes matched the partial 888-link/542-anchor receipt at resumption, with 828 worktree entries present. The [nondual method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md) remains unchanged. This round adopts one primary comparison and works through an explanatory discriminator; it adds no formal or empirical result.

### Identify the actual claim before naming a rival

The [relational argument](RELATION-AND-REASON-ARGUMENT.md) defends an objective contributory moral reason through the real difference a response could make to severe, still-unwanted adversity. Its comparison with a prior-role restriction remains in place. The [constitutive answer](CONSTITUTIVE-ANSWER-ARGUMENT.md) identifies the available response's answering that adversity as the proposed practical ground. Neither passage already asserts a completed reduction of every normative relation to nonnormative causal features.

The earlier [full-theory audit §6](HUIGEN-FULL-THEORY-AUDIT.md#6-can-scientific-universality-supply-the-missing-ethical-premises) expressly allows an independently justified irreducible normative premise as a possible route into the necessary root. This is an existing alternative, not a new relaxation introduced to accommodate the comparator. It still requires justification and practical indispensability. Calling a premise irreducible does not establish either, discharge the stronger no-assumptions objective, or excuse explaining its place in the unified account.

The [new primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#55-concrete-reasons-and-their-constitutive-explanation) distinguishes a concrete consideration's being a reason from a further explanation of normative significance. Unlike the deliberately matched P in §57, this is an independently developed metatheory. Unlike the earlier optative reconstruction, it permits objective, truth-apt normative claims. It does not thereby prove our specific moral minimum or qualify as a complete counterexample to the whole root.

### Work the same relief comparison through both accounts

Retain §57's actual experiencing continuity, actor, available response, relevant evidence and independently warranted permission and choice conditions. D includes its defended patient-good claim; it is not a neutral sensor description. Compare an option that would relieve the episode with one that would merely suppress its report while the episode persists. Fix the relevant costs and other consequences only to the extent required by this stipulated comparison. An intervention that actually changes the experience is a different case, not report masking by definition.

| Question | Generative candidate | Our reason-fundamentalist reconstruction |
|---|---|---|
| What occurrence and transformation are at issue? | The formed patient–response relationship identifies what would change and whose burden it is. | The same actual facts identify the consideration and its application circumstances. |
| Why does the relieving option have the proposed moral point? | G identifies this relation as a contributory moral ground under its stated conditions. | A warranted normative relation makes that concrete consideration count in favor under the same conditions. |
| What changes in the masking option? | It would not realize the stipulated relief, so that particular relief-ground is absent. | The purported relieving fact is false, so the same relief-based application fails. |
| What remains open? | Whether this constitutive account is warranted at the proposed scope and whether a further explanatory identity is necessary. | Warrant for the same moral application and for the claim that the relevant normative relation admits no further reduction. |

The right column applies the comparator's architecture to our stipulated moral target. It is not an attribution of a stranger-relief theorem to its author. Reasons fundamentalism by itself does not entail that target, its universal range or categorical rational authority.

The masking option may have a different benefit or reason. Nor does a failed stochastic outcome or nonexecution retrospectively erase an ex ante reason. This comparison concerns a means claim that would not deliver the specified relief. Matched approval or reports do not preserve the patient's actual condition.

The contrast tests whether an account treats helper approval as sufficient or exhaustive despite the stipulated patient-ground. It does not uniquely explain why genuine relief is reason-giving: both displayed accounts can preserve that difference. The inherited moral judgment supplies the application constraint; a second reconstruction is not independent evidence for its truth. The positive argument against an exclusive prior-role requirement does not automatically establish superiority over this different comparator.

### What would make the disagreement substantive?

Three claims must be kept distinct:

1. The relevant experiencing and responding circumstances form within actual activity.
2. Those circumstances supply this warranted moral reason.
3. A specified generative constitution exhaustively explains their reason-giving significance without retaining an irreducible normative relevance relation.

The third is stronger than the first two. If proposed, it needs a defended identity or explanatory derivation; a fundamentalist's contrary claim must address that same identity. Current G does not acquire the third commitment merely through the word “constitutes.” Conversely, normative vocabulary in D does not prove irreducibility: an adequate identity could explain the dependency immanently. The test must therefore trace what work the proposed account actually does, including where patient-good and permission obtain their warrant, rather than forbid such a derivation in advance.

A causal account of learning or a contrast between two outcomes may explain which circumstances occur, become known or change. It can contribute to a fuller explanation without settling all three claims. A normative account can explain what counts in favor without supplying the actual formation history. Coordinating those roles is a positive option within one unfolding; their analytical distinction does not posit an independent moral substance. The existing proof account likewise distinguishes a generated proof occurrence from its warranted inferential relations. Simply rejecting that distinction on the ethical side would require an argument.

### Consequence for the full necessity objective

H9's moral content is not refuted by a compatible account of why it holds. An alternative explanation, or omission of ours, is not automatically a denial of its truth. H12 concerns the warranted joint basis and valid generation under a faithful interpretation, rather than mandatory use of one vocabulary or explanatory route. A genuine disagreement must identify a commitment the theory actually asserts and preserve its intended force and domain. Selecting a translation that fails while another independently faithful joint interpretation succeeds would not refute the stated containment target.

The [central update](HUIGEN-FULL-THEORY-AUDIT.md#12-reason-making-circumstances-and-explanatory-necessity) records this application of the existing necessity criterion. This round neither establishes the comparator's full adequacy nor proves that every account admits the required interpretation. It also does not replace the stronger generative foundation with a collection of unexplained normative premises.

**Next central connection:** specify the constitutive claim the unified root actually needs and trace its patient-good, eligibility and normative-relevance dependencies within the same unfolding. Determine what further explanatory work it accomplishes beyond identifying the actual reason-making circumstance, and test any claimed identity against this substantive non-reductive account. A successful derivation would strengthen the root; a defended incompatibility or same-scope countercase would require revision. Original conditional acceptance is unchanged, and whole-root necessity remains open.


## 60. When generativity forms an additional life

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **generative formation → a newly realized worthwhile life → the ethical quality of the whole history**. The previous goal turn made progress by revalidating the original sound-generation package. Its 48 recorded artifact hashes match at resumption. This study returns to substantive grounding and applies the [whole-process direction](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#the-whole-as-the-grounding-question). C1/C2 remain local tests; they do not choose the foundation's starting divisions or its governing next question. The preceding continuation's proposed return to that older route is corrected here, without withdrawing its scoped results.

### The inherited comparisons do not yet cover this formation

The [future-bearer study](FUTURE-BEARER-FORMATION-ARGUMENT.md) compares A, R and B, each of which forms one worthwhile life. Its protection argument can retain its point when the intervention changes identity. It expressly leaves creation versus noncreation unresolved. The [whole-process comparison](WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#2-the-positive-constitutive-candidate) instead preserves relevant lives and capacities while removing impairment. Both are resources for the theory. Neither has yet established that forming an additional worthwhile life improves the whole.

The omitted condition matters: having a worthwhile future life in every alternative cannot silently become optional in an inference whose warrant depends on that common feature. This is a limit of the established comparisons, not an assertion that reality consists of independently existing persons. Generative activity can form the bearer as well as its good. Our description must then represent that change before deciding its ethical significance.

### Fix the two actual kinds of continuation

Consider two independently admitted continuations of one formative situation. In H₀ no additional experiencing life forms. In H₊ a new life p forms and realizes an independently defended worthwhile course of experience and activity. Both retain the specified goods and valid claims of existing lives. Actual formation, supporting conditions, opportunity costs, means, risks and ecological consequences must be assessed. The comparison assumes no countervailing warranted loss in these respects; it does not assert that all physical facts are identical or that creating a life consumes nothing. Unknown effects cannot be dismissed as zero.

No welfare level of zero is assigned to p’s absence in H₀. There is no p there whose life we have improved. H₀ and H₊ are nevertheless both histories of the unfolding under consideration. The question is whether the actual fulfillment newly realized in H₊ makes that whole history ethically better. Nonformation need not be deprivation, an injury, a frustrated claim or an existing person's loss for that comparative question to be meaningful.

Keep four claims explicit:

| Claim | What it concerns |
|---|---|
| L: p's realized life is good for p | The quality of the actual life in H₊ |
| I: H₊ contains that realized good | Faithful inclusion of the occurrence in the whole |
| B: H₊ is ethically better than H₀ because of it | The disputed comparison of the two whole histories |
| R: an agent has a reason or duty to bring about H₊ | Practical significance, means and authority; a reason and a duty are themselves distinct |

L together with actual realization supports I. Neither descriptive inclusion nor the word generative establishes B. B also must not be substituted for an independently justified practical requirement. This study tests B; it does not use R as a proxy. It makes no claim that new lives are exchangeable with existing lives or that goodness is an additive population total.

### A substantive restriction on whole comparison

[Bader's inspected argument](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#56-forming-an-additional-life-and-comparing-whole-histories) combines personal noncomparability with a further reduction of general betterness to personal good. His neutrality is incomparability, not equality. His note 9 reserves an additional argument about noncomparative creation reasons. We therefore use the stated restriction on whole comparison, not a denial of duty, as the relevant challenge.

The conceptual point is precise. Failure of a personal comparison does not, by itself, show that two whole histories lack the relata required for their comparison. A further account must explain why their ethical comparison is exhausted by that personal relation. Conversely, retaining two whole histories in the representation does not prove that one is better. The theory must warrant the comparative content; calling it immanent or whole supplies no ranking.

This opponent need not make a person a primitive substance. A person-affecting ethical restriction can be stated about lives formed within one generative unfolding. Non-self cannot defeat it merely by rejecting independent ownership. It must be assessed for how it understands the significance of the life actually realized and the reach of comparison across the alternatives.

### The positive case and its present limit

Our favored extension gives positive comparative significance to this benign realization: the additional history contains an actual worthwhile life, with the specified existing goods retained and no countervailing warranted loss. The favorable consideration is the lived fulfillment itself, not an increase in production, a satisfied demand from a nonexistent person or repayment to the initiator. It can end without producing further good. This is compatible with the nondual starting point because both the differentiated bearer and its life arise within the history being assessed.

The positive warrant is that the goodness already defended in the actual life need not lose its relevance to the whole merely because the process could have failed to form its bearer. A missing prior claimant explains why nonformation need not wrong anyone. It does not independently establish that realizing the good cannot improve the whole. This answers one proposed exclusion of B, while leaving a substantive person-affecting restriction to be assessed. It is a defended extension, not a deduction from L and I alone. Its additional ethical commitment is precisely that the newly realized good can improve the history across this change of bearer-domain.

[Harman's existing positive comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#56-forming-an-additional-life-and-comparing-whole-histories) supplies a different kind of support: benefits can count in favor of permissible creation without making creation obligatory. Its reasoning concerns practical justification. It cannot establish B simply by being cited as support for R. Nor does the absence of a duty establish neutrality or incomparability.

Three possible findings would change the account. A defect in the claimed good or an overlooked burden would revise this particular comparison. An independently warranted restriction on whole betterness could defeat B while retaining L, I and prior protection arguments. A successful defense of B would extend the proposed generative goodness to this case, but would still need a separate necessity argument before entering the indispensable joint root. No total ranking, creation mandate or unlimited expansion principle follows merely from the extension.

### Consequence for 慧根

This is a test of a strengthened reading of generative goodness, not a newly imposed completion condition or a counterexample to the broader current candidate. The existing theory has deliberately left B open. A scientific philosophy that rejects B has not thereby contradicted a commitment that our root already established. If we adopt B as part of that root, we must defend its warrant and indispensability under a faithful interpretation.

The advance is a concrete new scope test and identification of the additional ethical commitment it needs. The governing next connection is **the actual good of a newly formed life → comparative quality of the whole formation history**. Test the substantive restriction on that comparison, preserving genuine goods and losses, before deriving particular actors' reasons. The conditional foundation and the newly revalidated finite proofs retain their established scope; the full necessary-root objective remains open.

## 61. Affirmative comparison across the formation of a life

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **actual worthwhile formation → warranted comparison with nonformation → strict whole-history improvement**. The previous turn made progress by distinguishing a newly formed life's good from the betterness of its whole history. At resumption, its passing receipt's 48 artifact hashes still matched; 868 worktree entries reflected concurrent research. The present study tests the substantive restriction introduced in [§60](#60-when-generativity-forms-an-additional-life), then develops a conditional positive route. The whole-process method governs throughout. This is an application of elementary order reasoning, not a novel general theorem or an assumption-free derivation of value.

### The rival survives the existing comparisons

A restriction on comparison need not erase the life, deny its good, or posit an independently existing owner. Our substantive reconstruction says that comparative whole goodness requires an adequate life-to-life comparison covering both histories. An additional life has no counterpart in nonformation. That coverage condition is contested ethical content; the mere absence of a personal welfare baseline does not establish it. It nevertheless explains the restriction rather than freely toggling a goodness label in a thinner ontology.

One finite coherence witness retains each complete history and its independently warranted comparisons, grouped by the number of lives formed anywhere in the assessed history. Within each equal-number group, retain a transitive comparison relation, including comparisons between different identities when warranted. Leave different-number groups incomparable. The union remains transitive: every two-step comparison stays within one group. A death does not delete a life from the assessed history, and a change of identity alone need not change its group. No scalar total or welfare for absence is required.

This witness tests the consistency of the named commitments. It does not certify every proposed within-group judgment, the coverage restriction's moral truth, or a complete alternative to the generative foundation. A finite history description must still preserve formation, means, relations, claims and consequences relevant to its judgment. The classification does not establish that every imagined alternative is physically available. Moreover, the existing future-bearer protection argument often establishes a contributory consideration, not a strict ordering of complete histories. It therefore supplies even less reason to infer a contradiction here.

The [primary comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#57-incomparability-and-affirmative-addition) distinguishes equal-number comparison, value-differences and whole value-levels. It also records a direct response to the claim that incomparability is incoherent because it can interrupt a combined comparison. Incomparability cannot simply be treated as a removable, value-free contribution.

### A positive route with its actual premise exposed

Fix three independently admitted whole histories from the relevant formative situation:

| History | Retained difference |
|---|---|
| H₀ | No additional experiencing life forms. |
| H₁ | An additional life p forms and has an independently defended worthwhile continuation. |
| H₂ | The same p forms, with a continuation independently judged a strict whole improvement over H₁. |

H₁ and H₂ must retain the relevant formation conditions, existing goods, authority, means and wider effects. For example, a specified avoidable burden in p's otherwise worthwhile life might be removed without a countervailing warranted loss. This is a conditional case specification, not evidence that every burden admits such relief. A merely local improvement, preference change or favorable protection reason is insufficient to supply the strict whole comparison. No welfare coordinate is assigned to p's absence in H₀.

Let X ≽ Y mean that **X is at least as good as Y** in the normative whole-history relation under assessment. This is not a record of which comparisons we happen to have established. Assume the relation is reflexive and transitive over the admitted histories, without assuming completeness. Define its strict part by X ≻ Y exactly when X ≽ Y and not Y ≽ X. The particular premises below require independent warrant; a strict comparison requires exclusion of the reverse relation, not merely lack of evidence for it. The sufficient route is:

\[
H_1\succeq H_0,\qquad H_2\succ H_1
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad H_2\succ H_0.
\]

**Hand proof.** Transitivity gives H₂ ≽ H₀. If H₀ ≽ H₂, then H₁ ≽ H₀ ≽ H₂, contradicting H₂ ≻ H₁. Hence H₂ ≻ H₀. Antisymmetry is unnecessary; the strict-part definition supplies the required exclusion. These are alternative histories, not instructions first to create a burden and then relieve it.

The new ethical premise is H₁ ≽ H₀. It is stronger than merely denying H₀ ≻ H₁: incomparable histories satisfy that denial too. Neither an absence of objections, a lack of duty to refrain, nor uncertainty about which history is worse supplies affirmative weak comparison. This is one sufficient route to H₂'s strict betterness, not a uniquely minimal premise or a necessary route to that conclusion. It does not prove strict addition for H₁.

### How far the conditional route generalizes

For a fixed H₀, let A be a nonempty class of admitted worthwhile additional-life histories. Write W for the claim that every H in A satisfies H ≽ H₀, and S for the claim that every H in A satisfies H ≻ H₀. S immediately implies W.

To infer S from W using this argument, supply a further condition: **every target H in A has another K in A forming the same additional bearer under the required retained circumstances, with H ≻ K**. Each target then inherits strict betterness over H₀ from the hand argument. Under this condition, W and S are equivalent. Calling the universal premise merely weak would therefore conceal its full force once that condition is accepted.

The condition is not automatic. It does not follow from generativity, a capacity for learning, or mathematical continuity of an analyst's score. A worthwhile life might have no admitted strictly lower worthwhile counterpart. A finite nonempty class cannot satisfy it: the equivalence-class quotient of a finite preorder is a finite partial order with a minimal element. Without this condition, the argument applies only to targets with the required comparator. A coherent three-history ordering can tie H₁ with H₀ while putting H₂ strictly above both. W can then hold while S fails. Conversely, the condition is not necessary for S itself; another warranted route could establish strict addition.

### The remaining ethical adjudication

The positive generative proposal favors H₁ ≽ H₀ because the specified formation realizes actual good while retaining other warranted goods and introducing no countervailing warranted loss. That proposal assesses the differentiated whole, with no prior deprived claimant or return to the initiator. The rival asks why this warrants a whole comparison when one history contains a life without a counterpart. Answering by simply calling the formation harmless supplies only a negated objection unless a substantive nonworsening principle is defended. Answering that every occurrence belongs to the whole supplies inclusion, not comparison.

The study therefore closes a particular attempted shortcut and opens a precise constructive route. Same-number improvement and transitivity do not refute addition-incomparability. Affirmative benign addition, together with a warranted improvement, does yield a strict favorable whole comparison. Extending that route to every worthwhile formation requires an independently defended domain condition, under which the apparently weaker universal premise has the original claim's strength.

**Next connection:** assess the proposed ground of affirmative benign addition itself against the substantive coverage restriction, including its treatment of actual formation and retained differentiated goods. A successful defense must explain why the comparison is warranted, not merely show that it can be consistently represented. If it fails, revise the extension while preserving actual life-good and earlier protection. The necessary-root claim cannot yet disqualify the rival as unscientific. No creation duty, population maximization, replacement permission, new Lean result or empirical validation follows.

## 62. From worthwhile continuations to a formation boundary

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **differentiated whole histories → a warranted limiting comparison → affirmative addition**. Section 61 established a sufficient preorder argument while leaving its ethical crossing premise open. This section tests a proposed route to that premise. It applies elementary order reasoning and the distinctions documented in [source §58](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#58-whole-histories-and-the-warrant-for-limit-comparisons). It adds no new formal verification, empirical study or assumption-free derivation.

### What extension preserves, and what it changes

Extending an existing life can realize further fulfillment while preserving its bearer. Its justification can also involve continued projects, relationships and avoidance of truncation. Forming another life changes those relations. Calling both developments extensions of generative activity does not establish that their comparative grounds are interchangeable. The analogy between adding a time interval to a life and adding a life to a history therefore needs a substantive argument, not a change of vocabulary.

The nondual starting point preserves this distinction. Lives and their continuities form within the unfolding; they are not independent substances to be added from outside. But actual differentiation remains relevant. Describing the entire history supplies common objects of comparison without already warranting an ethical ordering across changes in who exists.

### The limiting histories must be the right histories

Consider an independently admitted sequence H₁, H₂, …, each forming a worthwhile additional life, and a proposed limit H₀ in which that additional life never forms. For the extension-based application, the positive members preserve the same bearer and the relevant background conditions. Any variation of identity requires its own justification. H₀ contains no absent person's welfare coordinate.

Suppose the experienced continuation becomes progressively shorter. This alone does not establish convergence to H₀. Formation activity, support commitments, environmental changes and effects on existing lives may remain. The limit could be an aborted-formation history rather than the specified nonformation alternative. A duration projection can converge while the complete relevant histories do not.

The proposed convergence structure must therefore be independently justified for these histories and preserve their ethically relevant distinctions. Physical availability is also a separate condition. A minimum realization interval, different formation process or persistent burden can defeat this particular construction. Generativity does not imply arbitrarily divisible lives or continuity of every evaluative relation.

### A conditional positive route

Let ≽ denote the normative whole-history relation under assessment, assumed reflexive and transitive. Its strict part satisfies X ≻ Y exactly when X ≽ Y and not Y ≽ X. These are claims about the relation, not about which judgments investigators presently accept.

Suppose the following are independently warranted:

1. The actual admitted histories satisfy H₁ ≽ H₂ ≽ H₃ ≽ … .
2. This sequence genuinely converges to the specified H₀.
3. Weak betterness obeys the relevant **transfinite-transitivity rule**: the monotone comparison carries to this limit, yielding H₁ ≽ H₀.
4. An admitted target H* is strictly better than H₁ under the retained conditions.

Then H* ≻ H₀. Ordinary transitivity yields H* ≽ H₀. If H₀ ≽ H*, transitivity with H₁ ≽ H₀ would give H₁ ≽ H*, contradicting H* ≻ H₁. The strict conclusion concerns H*, not automatically every worthwhile formation. Neither a merely contributory protection reason nor longer duration by itself supplies the whole-history premises.

Kowalczyk's monotone-chain route must be distinguished from a stronger continuity formulation. One sufficient alternative fixes a comparator K, assumes K ≽ Hₙ for every n, and requires its lower contour {Y : K ≽ Y} to be closed at H₀. This gives K ≽ H₀; a separately warranted H* ≻ K gives the strict result. General fixed-comparator continuity implies the relevant chain rule with ordinary transitivity, but the converse need not hold. The stronger condition is not a necessary premise of the narrower route. Neither follows merely from physical convergence.

### What the substantive rival can reject

A scoped coherence comparison makes the disputed commitment visible. Suppose admitted histories Hₜ, with 0 < t ≤ 1, have independently warranted comparisons Hₜ ≽ Hₛ exactly when t ≥ s, while H₀ compares only with itself. Here t indexes a specified history family; it is not a welfare value assigned to existence or absence. The relation is transitive and permits strict improvement among positive members while leaving formation and nonformation incomparable.

If these histories converge to H₀ as t decreases to zero, the relation fails the proposed limit-preservation rule. The coverage rival restricts whole betterness to cases supported by adequate personal-comparison coverage. This is a substantive proposed ground for the boundary, not simply an arbitrary label toggle or a claim that people are independent substances. It can thus retain the stipulated extension comparisons and reject that ethical rule at the formation boundary. Alternatively, it can contest the claimed history limit. This is a substantive disagreement rather than a contradiction exposed by ordinary transitivity. The construction establishes coherence only; it does not certify physical realization, the ethical restriction or a complete alternative foundation.

### Consequence for the necessary-root inquiry

An infinite family does not require an agent to perform infinitely many comparisons. A finite argument can establish a quantified family and apply a finitely stated limit rule. Even infinitely many valid finite consequences, considered without a warranted rule connecting them to the limit, do not establish the limiting comparison. Checking finitely many examples also does not establish the universal premises. Sound generation must preserve this difference.

The positive gain is an exact route that could justify affirmative addition without a cardinal sum or welfare for nonexistence. Its remaining obligations concern both the complete-history limit and the ethical rule carrying comparison to it. H12 therefore requires assessing the interpretation and warrant of that rule before treating its consequences as necessities.

Affirmative addition remains an optional strengthening of the current whole-process candidate, not a new completion gate. This formation comparison also does not settle mutual-benefit helping between already formed lives. Its unresolved premise cannot be imported as a prerequisite for that different case. For this optional extension, the next task is to defend or revise the particular limit-preservation commitment. The central foundation continues to investigate whole quality, differentiated significance and the formation of warranted concern and wisdom; it need not wait for this extension. Actual fulfillment, the mutual-benefit helping comparison and the accepted conditional foundation retain their scopes.

## 63. Developing purposes and the unit of warranted inquiry

6 October 2026. AI-authored continuation through **shared activity → developing understanding and purposes → warranted inquiry and improvement**. The question is how development can be justified before its participants can rank every possible future life. [Audit §27](#27-warranted-acquisition-and-the-direction-of-justification) already permits warranted learning without complete appreciation, separates reasonable failure from lucky success, and distinguishes discovery from formation. Sections 43 and 52–55 already preserve changing grounds, widened perspectives, purpose revision and continuing claims. The present addition concerns the unit of prospective justification and the conditions for composing its conclusions.

### A genuinely beneficial activity with an open future

Consider a musician and a novice entering limited shared musical practice. The novice develops an actual expressive capacity; the musician gains genuine enjoyment and understanding through responding to another's interpretation. Helping benefits both overall in the specified comparison, while changing the musician's way of living. These benefits require their own identification and defense. They are not inferred merely from participation, greater activity or subsequent enthusiasm.

The musician need not already know whether a life centered on teaching would be better than every available life of solo performance. The joint activity can develop purposes neither participant represented completely beforehand. Difference from an earlier plan is not itself a loss. Nevertheless, valuable solo activities may be forgone, fatigue can matter, and either participant may legitimately decline further involvement. The shared perspective preserves both gains and these possible conflicts without adding private scores or creating one encompassing experiencer.

The [current sibling account of formed appreciation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#formed-appreciation-and-added-good) constrains the inference. Fulfilled concern can be a good without its acquisition necessarily improving on continued informed indifference. Appreciation, pleasure and relationship may also articulate one realized good rather than three independently countable gains. Our stipulated beneficial case does not reverse that correction or establish every transformation as beneficial.

### What justifies an intermediate exercise?

An unfamiliar exercise might be valuable chiefly through its place in the shared inquiry: it exposes a difficulty, develops interpretive sensitivity or helps participants understand which form of cooperation fits their capacities. They need not separately desire that exercise as a final end. But its role must be real, its costs proportionate, and its authority appropriate to the actual undertaking.

The inquiry concerns whether and how the practice should develop. It does not authorize a predetermined destination such as permanent teaching, dependence on this musician, or eventual endorsement of every exercise. Evidence of exhaustion, misplaced instruction or a better alternative can support changing or ending it. A decision to stop can be an intelligible outcome of successful inquiry rather than proof that the earlier inquiry lacked warrant.

This supplies a positive conditional argument. Independently warranted mutual goods and worthwhile inquiry can support a bounded contribution whose relation to them is adequately understood, even when the eventual ordering of life possibilities is not. The rationale belongs to the exercise within an actual continuing activity. It need not be reconstructed as an isolated choice of an entire transformed life. Nor does a description of an extended project establish the worth of undertaking it: the relevant goods, inquiry significance, means and competing claims remain justified premises.

The [Paul–Callard dispute](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#59-prospective-guidance-within-a-transforming-activity) sharpens this limit. Paul challenges rational authorization where no persisting higher-order value supports transformation; dividing it into small steps does not settle that problem. Callard proposes understanding aspiration through an ongoing inquiry directed by partial features of an incompletely grasped target. The present case may satisfy Paul's easier authorization conditions. It therefore illustrates a positive route without refuting her harder objection, proving that proleptic reasons are necessary, or treating an inquiry analogy as a complete account of guidance.

A comparison can preserve genuine mutual gains and fully informed eventual endorsement while varying the guidance available during formation. In one history, identified partial understanding and a proportionate inquiry support the encounter; in another, a commitment reaches the same beneficial destination without adequate contemporaneous grounds. Under the stated warrant difference, the favorable endpoint does not erase the difference in rational guidance. This applies §27’s existing distinction between reasonable inquiry and lucky success to the organization of stages; it is not a new claim that one endpoint must be worse, or a free reassignment of rationality to identical histories. The actual evidence, undertaking and relation of the encounter to its question must differ as specified.

### Three conclusions that do not compose automatically

Keep three assessments distinct:

| Assessment | What it establishes |
|---|---|
| There was adequate reason to undertake this step. | Its prospective practical warrant under the available evidence and conditions. |
| This step realized an independently identified good. | A favorable aspect of the actual activity. |
| The resulting whole continuation is better than the relevant alternative. | A comparative judgment retaining the other goods, losses and claims. |

The first can hold despite failure, and the second despite an unjustified gamble. Neither automatically supplies the third. Repeating either assessment across several steps therefore does not establish a monotonically improving whole trajectory. Changing the purpose against which success is assessed makes that inference especially hazardous: an earlier course assessed by its original aim and a later course assessed only by the aim it produces do not yet form one comparative chain.

The [inherited burden–repair comparison](WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#7-continuation-the-origin-of-a-need-and-the-value-of-its-repair) supplies a concrete warning. Once a burden exists, relief can be justified and genuinely beneficial without vindicating the activity that needlessly produced and then relieved it. Shared caring fulfillment does not remove the entrance-history comparison. This is not a new counterexample, and it does not deny the real mutual benefit stipulated in the musical case. It shows why a reached-condition justification cannot silently substitute for appraisal of the formation history.

Partial comparisons can nevertheless compose. If independently warranted comparisons concern the appropriate complete alternatives under one transitive whole-history relation, a finite chain supports its corresponding conclusion without ranking every other possibility. That relation can assess purposes, capacities and relationships which genuinely develop. It is not a frozen person's original taste. Its applicability, retained grounds and interpretation must be preserved across the chain; a sequence of changing personal endorsements alone supplies none of these conditions.

### What valid generation must preserve

This applies H12 to distinct kinds of claim; it does not correct a theorem claiming that every process event is moral progress. The [earlier reliability study](#34-experiential-reliability-and-the-necessity-of-generated-claims) already preserves fallibility. An inquiry act, a conjecture and an established result have different statuses. A warranted investigation may produce a hypothesis needing further assessment. A new relationship may supply a new ground rather than discover an unchanged one. A sound derivation must retain those distinctions instead of promoting every developmental output to a necessary truth.

The root can therefore support open development without a completed future ranking. Its particular inferences still require warranted premises and appropriate rules. The same ongoing activity can form understanding, change what is good or owed, and revise its purposes; these achievements must be identified rather than collapsed into the fact that the process continued.

The next central question is the warrant of the first partial grasp: which identifiable feature of actual realization supports the learner's orientation and its correction? A clearer inquiry structure can expose that question, but cannot answer it by predicting eventual approval. Nor must every actual benefit await a new procedure before mattering. The whole-process candidate, the mutual-benefit case and the accepted conditional foundation retain their scopes while the stronger grounding and necessity claims remain open.

## 64. Mutual development as a test of ethical grounding

7 October 2026. AI-authored synthesis of Ren's [non-self correction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-non-self-and-a-perspective-including-both.md), §§52–53 and 63, under the [normative-grounding priority](NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md). The transition under investigation is **shared development → justified ethical quality → practical reason**. This round sharpens the inference the mutual-benefit case can support; it does not claim to discover its earlier reconstruction anew. The successful-pursuit impairment case remains a separate investigation and does not replace this case.

### The changed life can be part of the good

Retain the specified comparison: helping benefits both people overall relative to the relevant alternative, and changes the helper's way of living. In §63's example, shared musical practice develops the novice's expression and the musician's enjoyment and understanding. The musician's activity, concerns and direction can develop through responding. These are differentiated consequences within the same history, not consequences first assigned to independent proprietors and subsequently traded.

There is therefore no warrant for recording departure from the musician's earlier plan as an additional loss merely because it is a departure. Actual lost practice, an unfulfilled undertaking or a compromised ability to direct a life would be further considerations; their significance must be identified. Even where some valuable feature is forgone, the stipulated overall benefit to the helper must remain. The case cannot be recast as an overall sacrifice to obtain a familiar conflict.

The affirmative interpretation is stronger than compensation: the helping can partly constitute the worthwhile life now being formed. It need not first injure a finished self and then repay that injury. The other's development remains an actual good in the continuation, including aspects the helper never experiences or receives back. Their interaction may constitute one relational good articulated in two lives; counting each description as a separate increment would require justification.

This is an appraisal from within the developing relation. It requires neither one combined experiencer nor a privileged observer entitled to decide for both. Descriptions of helper and helped track particular differences in activity and consequence; those roles can change without making their actual histories interchangeable.

### What the stipulation establishes, and what it leaves open

The example grants genuine benefits. It therefore supplies a positive application of the candidate ethical account: those realized benefits favor helping, subject to the independently defended connection between good and practical reason. Bare life-shape change does not cancel that favor. This is more than a prediction of new motivation or later endorsement.

But the same stipulation cannot independently establish why these realizations are good. If “benefit” already means an improvement in a life, concluding that beneficial generation is good has not derived its evaluative content. Conversely, replacing benefit with merely observable novelty, continuation or coordination removes part of the original case. Neither maneuver resolves the grounding question.

The target is to explain why the whole activity's actual expression, understanding and responsive participation have the claimed ethical quality, including how the helper's transformed direction belongs to that quality. This does not require proving the intrinsic value of each isolated component before considering the whole. It does require a substantive defense of this realization, with its differentiated effects retained. The conceptual case is not empirical confirmation of that defense.

### A rival that preserves the relational reconstruction

A serious rival can accept formed selves, actual mutual improvement and the value of the shared practice, while permitting another worthwhile continuation. It can defend choosing solo creation through its particular expressive value and the practical significance of directing a life, rather than through an immutable ego or a net welfare loss. Those grounds themselves need justification; asserting autonomy is not a completed argument.

Such a rival need not deny a reason to help. It can dispute whether that reason requires this life transformation, or whether anyone has authority to impose it. A permission need not mean that declining is better. Our [Pummer comparison](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#60-mutual-development-and-the-grounding-limit) supplies a concrete version of this challenge without requiring metaphysically separate selves. The proposed relational pluralist reconstruction is ours, not a claim that Pummer endorses the whole generative theory.

The comparison consequently separates three questions: what makes this development good; what reason that good gives; and whether it excludes the other continuation. Non-self changes how the grounds are understood. It does not settle all three just by changing the description. This protects genuine development without silently treating every old preference as binding or every new caring identity as authoritative.

### Integrated source assessment and next discriminator

Wang's account of actual care and self-cultivation offers a positive constitutive comparison: their realization belongs to one activity. His innate-benevolence and heavenly-nature claims are substantive grounds in that account, not results established by our rereading. Pummer's feature-choice account preserves reasons for aid while defending some permissions among valuable life possibilities. The [source assessment](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#60-mutual-development-and-the-grounding-limit) records exactly what was reread and inherited. Neither historical affinity nor a philosophical counterexample supplies empirical validation.

A test claiming to ground that ethical quality must identify and defend the shared realization without taking “both benefit” as the premise that settles its value; the present scoped application may retain that stipulation. The discretion rival above already grants goodness and practical favor, so it tests obligation and permission, not first goodness or the good-to-reason connection. The first-grounding comparison needs a distinct rival that preserves the actual shared realization while disputing its proposed ethical identification. These tests must not substitute for one another. Evidence that the alleged expression or understanding is misidentified would revise its application. An independently defended worthwhile alternative or protected choice could limit obligation without defeating its goodness. Mere change from a former plan, mere preference for the new identity, and mere continuation of the activity settle neither question.

**Integrated result:** the present case is a positive application and a correction to treating life transformation as an automatic cost. It is not an independent proof of goodness as generativity or a refutation of every relationally grounded permission. All analytic distinctions concern relations within one unfolding. Existing conditional results remain accepted; universal normative grounding and full-root necessity remain open. No new formal or empirical result is claimed.

## 65. Relational final value and the universal argument

7 October 2026. AI-authored investigation of a possible route through **actual understanding → noninstrumental value → practical significance for any intelligence**. This addresses the necessary ethical-grounding inference in the [universal programme](NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md#the-affirmative-route-and-its-decisive-inference); it does not replace that objective with a local musical comparison. The preceding §64 clarified why the mutual-benefit stipulation could not independently prove its own value premise. This round evaluates a positive explanation through achievement and a proposed rival, then declines to promote their limited disagreement into a first-goodness result.

### The affirmative route through understanding

Within the shared musical activity, contributions can become intelligible through the participants' acquired capacities. A participant does more than repeat a correct instruction when they grasp how a change in timing alters the joint expression and use that grasp in responding. Their ability, its formation through instruction and practice, the actual expressive relation and its continuing effects belong to one history. A bounded attribution of understanding preserves this explanatory relation; it need not imply self-sufficient production or an independently constituted knower.

The positive appraisal concerns that actual exercise and realization. When a grasp succeeds through the relevant capacity, what the participants achieve can be worth realizing in its doing, without a separate later payoff. This is a substantive claim about the worthwhile character of such participation, not the claim that an activity first serves an arbitrary purpose and then acquires value merely from success. It explains what the favorable judgment tracks: an intelligible, effective contribution within the shared activity.

[Pritchard's argument](HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#61-understanding-achievement-and-welfare-making-explanation) supplies a precise philosophical precedent. He connects understanding to cognitive achievement and achievement to final value. Final means valuable for its own sake; it need not mean valuable independently of relationships. How a success is achieved can be part of the proposed ground. His argument motivates that value through comparison of achievement and mere success, while leaving the value judgment substantive. It allows qualifications for trivial or wicked achievements. We do not attribute to it a proof that all understanding has positive ethical value in every whole continuation.

This supports a possible constitutive explanation and locates its evaluative commitment. It does not derive the worth of achievement from generativity alone. Nor can the explanation be generalized by substituting all knowledge for understanding: Pritchard's testimony case distinguishes knowing an explanation from grasping it. Receiving knowledge through another person's contribution is not thereby worthless or nonintelligent. The proposed relation is one mode of realization, not an admission test for every good or every intelligence.

### The proposed rival answers a narrower question

Crisp's explanatory hedonism asks what makes something good for the person living it. He can include enjoyable achievement among goods while holding that enjoyment supplies its welfare-making character. This is more specific than saying that only experiences exist, or that relationships merely look real. It disputes an independent participant-benefit explanation while expressly allowing nonhedonic aesthetic or ethical value. Therefore it cannot, without an additional argument of our own, serve as a denial of the whole activity's ethical quality.

His isolation challenge also has a bounded force. Removing enjoyment and finding no benefit would not by itself establish that actual achievement contributes nothing when enjoyment is present. Crisp acknowledges the logical possibility of a jointly constituted good and asks its defender for an explanation. Our whole-grounding method permits such constitution, but that permission does not establish its truth. No requirement to certify each isolated constituent's intrinsic value is introduced.

The two primary positions consequently need not contradict each other. Understanding could have final value while adding no nonhedonic participant welfare. A further inference is needed to identify either with the whole ethical appraisal. This distinction concerns different questions about the same unfolding, not separately existing kinds of value-owning thing.

### Why another collaboration comparison is not yet decisive

Review proposed comparing two actual skilled collaborations: one with competent coordination and mutual regard, another adding explanatory grasp that guides reciprocal revision. Neither is a simulation, and both already contain achievement. The target would have to be the additional grasp or its integration, rather than achievement versus its complete absence. Matching enjoyment would also have to preserve qualitative enjoyment, not only a rating or duration. Feasibility of that preservation is not established by naming the cases.

The genealogy prevents overstating this proposal. [Whole-grounding §§12–18](WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#12-ordinary-realization-and-the-ground-of-excellence) already examine reciprocal expression, understanding and appreciative formation; [§32's adjudication](WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#adjudication-of-the-proposed-discriminator) allows relation-inclusive experience to explain the same actual good. No extra quantity of organizational value is required. A new collaboration vignette would therefore need a genuinely disputed explanatory dependence before it could discriminate those accounts.

We do not add a universal rule that extra understanding always improves the whole or benefits every participant. We also do not strengthen the theory merely to create an opponent. An understanding-sensitive ethical explanation remains a candidate to defend; a welfare-only objection cannot replace that defense or refute it by answering another question. The proposed comparison is not adopted as a new central test at its present specification.

### What follows for the universal claim

The attempted route has three separate obligations. First, explain the normative worth of the actual relational achievement, including contextual qualifications. Second, justify how that worth bears on a feasible response by an intelligence whose current purposes do not include it. Third, show that the required explanation depends on the generative theory at the scope asserted, rather than merely being compatible with it. Pritchard's achievement account supplies a substantive proposal for the first; Crisp disputes a particular welfare explanation. Neither source supplies the second or establishes the third.

This is a scoped adjudication of an attempted derivation, not a new impossibility theorem. Earlier epistemic-to-practical audits already establish that domain transfer needs an argument. The new comparison prevents final value, participant benefit and whole ethical force from being treated as interchangeable supports for that transfer. Its positive contribution is a relation-dependent account of noninstrumental achievement whose normative premise is visible, rather than another claim that organized activity is automatically good.

**Next:** investigate why the actual whole realization warrants favorable appraisal and how that warrant bears on an intelligence with actual understanding and response opportunity but no prior commitment to promote it. A conditional test of practical reach can advance one necessary link; success there would not independently discharge the first-goodness obligation. Preserve that intelligence's formation and effects within the whole. A rejection must be assessed for substantive misdescription, contradiction or failed ethical judgment; it cannot be classified as unintelligent by definition. The whole-grounding task remains open, and its missing warrant cannot be supplied by another comparison that already grants goodness. This returns to the universal inference with the present route's exact contribution and limits retained.

## Validation status

Elementary hand arguments only; no experiment, human-subject intervention or new Lean compilation was performed. Splitting and merger cases are stipulated conceptual cases, not claims that a particular technology realizes them. The note preserves the distinction between reasons, motivation, task correctness and moral authority. Repository links were checked after writing. The [conditional foundation is now accepted](FOUNDATION-ROOT-ACCEPTANCE.md) under the original five conditions. The stronger whole-root necessity objective remains open; earlier dated pending-status statements retain their historical scope.

**Independent cross-review, 6 October 2026:** no blocking issue was found in the complete generative-identity note, self-ground source note or [harm-identification note](HUIGEN-HARM-IDENTIFICATION.md). G1–G4's anchor, branch symmetry, identity-equivalence, scope-contraction and legitimate-role premises support their displayed conclusions. The continuity countermodel correctly fails the stronger inheritance premise. The sources distinguish faithful redescription from changing the reason's addressee; the terminal tests narrow a proposed functional ground without refuting every historical relation. A source-agent review of this audit's branching, succession and delegation arguments likewise found no blocker.

The selected eight-person/eleven-session description, nonsignificant intensity contrast, limited inference from withdrawal, and single-case reorganization caveat match those sources. No inference from these results to normative authority or a universal experiential classifier is made. This was bounded source and inference review, not reanalysis of participant data.
